# v1.1.0 BAMF-0.2 unified-history ledger

This ledger records the complete Git DAG included by release `v1.1.0`, including the BAMF-0.2 mainline bridge that joined the already-reviewed unified lineage to `main`. Ordinary linear release-note traversal did not enumerate that entire second-parent history.

## Coverage

- Range: [`v1.0.0...21a84db`](https://github.com/Entif-AI/Bilqis-MLX/compare/v1.0.0...21a84dba60b19027ae77fdfe4b722ff860a2f3f8)
- Complete DAG commits: **2,996**
- Commit subjects explicitly naming BAMF: **2,264**
- BAMF bridge PR: [#1029](https://github.com/Entif-AI/Bilqis-MLX/pull/1029), which joined 2,994 commits / 3,300 files into `main`
- Unified-tail bridge PR: [#1038](https://github.com/Entif-AI/Bilqis-MLX/pull/1038)

### Conventional-commit mix

| Type | Commits |
|---|---:|
| `feat` | 374 |
| `fix` | 767 |
| `perf` | 9 |
| `refactor` | 216 |
| `docs` | 645 |
| `test` | 298 |
| `build` | 1 |
| `ci` | 23 |
| `chore` | 199 |
| other / merge / non-conventional | 464 |
